AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='The fix is complete and verified. Here\'s a summary.\n\n## Root cause\n\n`ImageEditor.java` inferred "has the user saved?" by comparing two unrelated booleans:\n\n```java\nif (mClicked == mClickedFilter) { next(); ... } else { toast(save_first); }\n```\n\n`mClicked` meant "Save current was pressed" and `mClickedFilter` meant "a non-NONE filter is applied". Brush edits were tracked by a third flag (`mDoodleSelected`) that navigation never consulted. So for a brush-only edit: saving set `mClicked = true` while `mClickedFilter` stayed `false` → the flags diverged → the arrow button wrongly reported unsaved work. The same broken model also let a *genuinely* unsaved doodle be silently discarded (`false == false` → navigate), and allowed navigating away from an unsaved filter applied right after a save (`true == true`).\n\n## Change\n\nReplaced the flag pair with one accurately-maintained `mUnsavedChanges` flag:\n\n- **Set** when a non-NONE filter is applied, and — via `PhotoEditor.setOnPhotoEditorListener` — when a brush stroke is actually added/removed. Brush work is now tracked by real drawing events rather than by "brush mode was opened once". A `buildPhotoEditor()` helper attaches the listener at all three places the editor is rebuilt, so the callbacks survive the re-instantiation that happens on every filter/brush tap.\n- **Cleared** in `saveAsFile`\'s `onSuccess` (not on button press, so a failed save still blocks navigation) and in `resetCurrent()`.\n- **Checked** by both arrow handlers: `if (!mUnsavedChanges)` navigate, else toast.\n\nTwo small related defects fixed along the way, both in the path this bug exercises: `mFilterName` now defaults to `PhotoFilter.NONE.name()` (a brush-only save previously produced a file named `<timestamp>_null.jpg`, since it skips `applyFilter`), and `onSuccess` clears the overlay views once the doodle is baked into the saved bitmap — otherwise the strokes stayed on screen and rode along onto the next image, which becomes very visible now that navigating after a doodle-save actually works.\n\n## Verification\n\nTraced the reported repro against the new state machine: brush → draw (`onAddViewListener` → `true`) → Save current (`onSuccess` → `false`) → right arrow reads `false` → navigates, no toast. The warning still fires correctly for unsaved filters, and now also for unsaved doodles, which previously slipped through.\n\n`./gradlew clean assembleDebug` → **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**; `./gradlew app:checkstyle --rerun-tasks` → **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**.\n\nOne note: `git diff` is empty here, not because the edits didn\'t land but because this checkout lives under a `runs/` path that the enclosing repository\'s `.gitignore` excludes — the file itself is untracked. All changes are on disk in `app/src/main/java/swati4star/createpdf/activity/ImageEditor.java`, and I confirmed they\'re present after the build.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 2267, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 54891, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 2267, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 2, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfAHzL7Ji2QkE9rVBXQRL', stop_reason=None, session_id='55e91d40-334c-4318-a367-61887ddb2618', uuid='bab2cacd-b098-4692-82eb-c56330575038')
